Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the video structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 680. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 682 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on video structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 680. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 682. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the video structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 163. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 587 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 163 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the video structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 587. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
